- **handoff**: Manual command that carries work across a gap, in three modes. Default: compacts the current conversation into a handoff document a fresh session can pick up, and with --bg launches that session as a background agent. --catchup: rebuilds a returning human's context from live project state, read-only. --recover: recovers a session that died without a handoff, from a transcript digest. Not for restoring a conversation whole (claude --resume). ([Adminhead/claude-plugins](https://github.com/Adminhead/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/handoff/skills/handoff))

- **handoff**: Cria o handoff da sessão em docs/handoff/AAAA-MM-DD-&lt;assunto&gt;.md pelo template, com onde estamos, feito, em andamento, próximo passo concreto, ADRs criados, problemas e o resultado de scripts/verify.sh test rodado na hora, além da branch e dos commits sem push. Use ao encerrar uma sessão de trabalho. Não use para planejar, implementar ou registrar erro, e nunca para afirmar que está tudo funcionando sem a saída do verify. - **handoff**: Writes or updates HANDOFF.md at the repo root to capture in-progress work — goal, current state, findings and dead ends, decisions and their rationale, blockers, and the single next action. A request to stop the work for now, in whatever words or language, is not on its own a request for a handoff.
